<script type="text/javascript">
        //<![CDATA[
            var searchForm = new Varien.searchForm('search_mini_form', 'search', '');
            searchForm.initAutocomplete('https://www.itead.cc/search/ajax/suggest/', 'search_autocomplete');
        //]]>
        jQuery(document).on('click', function(event) {
            if (!jQuery(event.target).closest('.search-dropdown').length) {
                jQuery('.search-option-list').hide();
            }
        });
        jQuery(document).ready(function() {
            jQuery('.search-dropdown').click(function(event) {
                jQuery(this).children('.search-option-list').toggle(); 
                jQuery('.search-option-list span').click(function(){
                    var select = jQuery(this).text();
                    var vs = jQuery(this).parent("li").val();
                    jQuery('.search-option-list li').removeClass('selected');
                    jQuery(this).parent("li").addClass('selected');
                    jQuery(this).closest('.search-dropdown').children('.search-select').text(select);
                    jQuery('#cat-search').val(vs);
                });
            });
        });
        </script>
</form>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function close_topad(target){
        var parent = $one(target).attr('data-target');
        $one(target).parents(parent).remove();
    }

        function setGridItemsEqualHeight($)
    {
        var winWidth = $(window).width();
        var SPACING = 0;
        if (winWidth >= 480)
        {
            $('.show-grid').removeClass("auto-height");
            var gridItemMaxHeight = 0;
            $('.show-grid > .item').each(function() {
                $(this).css("height", "auto");
                                gridItemMaxHeight = Math.max(gridItemMaxHeight, $(this).height());
            });
            $('.show-grid > .item').css("height", gridItemMaxHeight + "px");
        }
        else
        {
            $('.show-grid').addClass("auto-height");
            $('.show-grid > .item').css("height", "auto").css("padding-bottom", "20px");
        }
    }
        function setGridItem($){
        var winWidth = $(window).width();
        var col = 3;
        if(winWidth > 768){
            newcol = col;
        }
        if(winWidth < 768 && winWidth >=640){
            newcol = 3;
        }
        if(winWidth >=480 && winWidth <640){
            newcol = 2;
        }
        if(winWidth < 480){
            newcol = 2;
        }
        $i = 0;
        $('.show-grid > .item').each(function() {
            $i++;
            $(this).removeClass('first');
            $(this).removeClass('last');
            if(($i-1)%newcol==0){
                $(this).addClass('first');
            }else if($i%newcol==0){
                $(this).addClass('last');
            }
        });
    }
    $one(function($) {
        var winWidth = $(window).width();
        var winHeight = $(window).height();
        var windowResize_t;
        $(window).resize(function() {
            var winNewWidth = $(window).width();
            var winNewHeight = $(window).height();
            if (winWidth != winNewWidth || winHeight != winNewHeight)
            {
                clearTimeout(windowResize_t);
                windowResize_t = setTimeout(function() {
                    $(document).trigger("themeResize");
                                        setGridItemsEqualHeight($);
                                        setGridItem($);
                }, 200);
            }
            winWidth = winNewWidth;
            winHeight = winNewHeight;
        });
                $('.one-maincompare').hover(

            },

            }
        );

                            }
        });
        //Back to top

                $('#back-top').fadeOut();

        });

        });

            $.colorbox({

                onComplete: function(){

                }
            });
